# Device parity review — 28 November 2026

This review covers SC-481, SC-482 and SC-483. ADI implementation and test references are pinned to `origin/main` at `a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0`. The task and its updated Shaper measurement requirement are at `c90c2bf3924f79de15cf8522f8ec47b0b083e2f8`. [Parity prompt](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/c90c2bf3924f79de15cf8522f8ec47b0b083e2f8/adi_daw/collab/prompts/2026-11-28-device-parity-lists.md#L44-L63) · [Sanity Check](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/c90c2bf3924f79de15cf8522f8ec47b0b083e2f8/adi_daw/docs/SANITY-CHECK.md#L684-L686)

`has` requires a working ADI path and an existing test that exercises the behavior. `part` means the implementation or test evidence covers only part of the feature. `missing` means the device has no implementation for the feature. `out` requires an explicit decision. Each test cell gives a small check for the full row. The Plan column names existing coverage for `part` and `missing` rows and uses “no row” for unassigned work. [Plan](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/docs/PLAN.md#L956-L982) · [Instrument parity contract](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/docs/DECISIONS.md#L11677-L11690)

Placements follow the proposed cards. Keep the primary control large, show the signal behind the curve, group short choices together and use the arrow to open detailed controls. Put occasional actions in the menu. The sheets govern placement and the code and tests establish what ships. [Sheet 6](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/b65fb0bac200689a9464168932e6e8731517ef4c/adi_daw/collab/window/design/proposals/devices-6.png) · [Sheet 7](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/b65fb0bac200689a9464168932e6e8731517ef4c/adi_daw/collab/window/design/proposals/devices-7.png) · [ADR-0243 d1](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/docs/DECISIONS.md#L18812-L18817) · [SC-455](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/docs/SANITY-CHECK.md#L658)

Verification on Linux Debug passed 14 headless CTests with Pd and warnings as errors, including the built-in device, Shaper, OneShot, Sampler, preset and acceptance suites. All six repository validators passed. The review inspected the existing JUCE window tests in source.

## SC-481 — ADI-Shaper and Cableguys ShaperBox 3.6

The clones build session measured **Cableguys ShaperBox 3.6**. The build log names the tested version, distinguishes it from the published v3.6.3 manual, and records **eleven Shapers** plus the separate **Compressor and Oscilloscope** tools. The v3.6 manual’s introduction says nine. Its contents and individual chapters enumerate eleven Shapers. Their names are Time, Pitch, Drive, Noise, Reverb, Filter, Liquid, Crush, Volume, Pan and Width. The 3.6 measurement contract confirms all eleven. [Build session reference and method](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/collab/clones.md#L1715-L1719), [first acceptance cases](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/collab/clones.md#L1795-L1808), [Cableguys manual, v3.6](https://downloads.cableguys.com/Cableguys-ShaperBox-3-Manual.pdf?v=3.6.1#page=2).

Main declares **418 controls** and processes **seven stages**, in order from Volume through Width, Pan, Filter, Sweep, Drive and Crush. Sweep is ADI’s name for the reference LiquidShaper’s flanger/phaser stage. A band has eight fixed values per contour and a shared mix and clock. Filter, Sweep and Crush each have two independent contours. The opened view in sheet 6 shows stage tabs, crossovers and the editing graph. The face shows a single selected stage and band with the large Master Mix. Placements below extend those rules for additional controls. [Pd parameter declarations](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/pd/devices/ADI-Shaper.pd#L11-L846), [core controls and contours](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/dsp/adi_shaper.hpp#L25-L54), [render order](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/dsp/adi_shaper.cpp#L790-L796), [ADR-0248 d11 and d14](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/docs/DECISIONS.md#L19388-L19395), [device sheet 6](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/b65fb0bac200689a9464168932e6e8731517ef4c/adi_daw/collab/window/design/proposals/devices-6.html#L23-L64).

DEV-31 incorporates the clones seat’s measured stage contract, transport, queued retriggers and a curve editor after sheet agreement. DEV-05 covers the host-side key route where noted. The standalone host modulation plan in DEV-07 covers modulators applied to device parameters. The clones log records a separate Width follower branch that is absent from main, and the internal per-band follower and its controls have no explicit PLAN row. [DEV-31](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/docs/PLAN.md#L3742-L3754), [DEV-05](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/docs/PLAN.md#L965), [DEV-07](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/docs/PLAN.md#L1095), [measured stage contract](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/collab/clones.md#L1721-L1750), [Width follower branch](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/collab/clones.md#L1208-L1216).

The director asks for a preset browser and a substantial collection of ADI-authored presets and templates. The v3.6 manual describes the browser’s categories, packs, tags and favorites and gives no fixed library count. [Director’s brief](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/c90c2bf3924f79de15cf8522f8ec47b0b083e2f8/adi_daw/collab/prompts/2026-11-28-device-parity-lists.md#L46-L60) · [Cableguys preset library](https://downloads.cableguys.com/Cableguys-ShaperBox-3-Manual.pdf?v=3.6.1#page=30)

### Sound comparisons on the seven implemented Shapers

The build session ran registered ADI-Shaper and ShaperBox 3.6 on the same stereo kick-and-bass samples at 48 kHz and 120 BPM, with editors closed. The seven-stage review measured four-second captures at 256 and 2,048 frames per callback after 98,304 warm-up frames. The session also used impulses, steady tones and moving shapes to isolate individual settings. The errors below belong to the named fixtures and build revisions. [Seven-stage review and measurement setup](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/collab/clones.md#L1153-L1175) · [Registered main comparison](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/collab/clones.md#L729-L747)

- **Volume.** On identical 48 kHz kick and bass, three bands at gains 0.35, 0.70 and 0.85 with 12 and 24 dB/octave splits had a maximum sample error of **0.000000477** over 192,000 frames per channel at each of 64, 256 and 1,024 frames per callback on the five-stage build. The held one-bar gate in the original three-stage build changed **16 samples earlier** than the reference and agreed within **0.00001** after shifting those edges. The first-order split repair now in main reduced the three-gain kick-and-bass error from **0.003218** to **0.000000021 RMS** on its own fixture. [Three-band gain test](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/collab/clones.md#L1645-L1649), [gate](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/collab/clones.md#L1681-L1691), [first-order split repair](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/collab/clones.md#L181-L187).
- **Width.** In the original stereo-input test, Width set to mono matched directly over 192,000 frames per channel. The seven-stage mono run agreed within **2.2 × 10⁻¹¹ RMS** per channel after compensating the reference’s 16-sample reserve. On the 48 kHz three-band kick-and-bass test, the first-order crossover repair now in main reduced Width error from **0.000212** to **0.000000043 RMS** at both callback sizes. [Mono at startup](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/collab/clones.md#L1681-L1691), [seven-stage mono](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/collab/clones.md#L1173-L1175), [split Width](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/collab/clones.md#L181-L187).
- **Pan.** The original balance-law test agreed within **0.0000002 in gain** on both channels at 32 held-out positions. The landed selectable-blend repair tested signed impulses at 44.1, 48 and 96 kHz and a moving contour, with a worst stereo-channel RMS error of **0.000000483** for the direct moving test. In the registered hard-switch kick-and-bass test with reference latency compensated, worst-channel RMS was **0.00001634**, **0.00005084** and **0.00001256** at 0%, 50% and 100% blend respectively across 256- and 2,048-frame callbacks. These results use the existing 2 ms delay range and standard balance law. [Held-out balance](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/collab/clones.md#L1693-L1699), [landed Pan repair](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/collab/clones.md#L619-L631), [delay alignment](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/collab/clones.md#L667-L673).
- **Filter.** The clean low-pass passed the 400 Hz and 8 kHz checkpoints and five-mode, small-signal stereo impulse error was below **0.000008**. The moving cutoff/resonance fixture differed by up to **0.000037** and a half-amplitude impulse at extreme resonance by up to **0.01655**. In a later 48 kHz test of the main kernel, the model retained an earlier marker across exact silence, changing the resumed peak by **0.00116021** in 12 dB low-pass and **0.00197680** in 12 dB high-pass. ADI’s paired runs were identical and its peak error without the marker was below **0.00000003**. [Five filter modes](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/collab/clones.md#L1657-L1665), [return from silence](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/collab/clones.md#L54-L58).
- **Sweep.** The build compared signed impulse responses while varying center and feedback. Across fifteen flanger settings, the first 5,000 normalized samples differed by less than **0.000075**. The highest tested phaser feedback retained a **0.0222** normalized tail discrepancy. The aligned kick-and-bass comparison on the registered seven-stage build at `87ea349c` reported a stereo residual below **0.00000179 RMS** for the selected Sweep setting. [Flanger and phaser impulses](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/collab/clones.md#L1657-L1665) · [Registered main comparison](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/collab/clones.md#L729-L745)
- **Drive.** Across three implemented styles, signed impulse probes at 44.1, 48 and 96 kHz differed by at most **0.000292**, with matched peaks at sample 16. Six independent 199 Hz tones at three input levels agreed within **0.000057** on the tested harmonics, and a full-drive 9 kHz hard-clip probe agreed within **0.000031** on measured alias amplitudes. The seven-stage kick-and-bass case gave **0.00003535** worst-channel RMS and both units reported 16 samples. The four-instance optimization now on main preserved the preceding build’s stereo captures byte for byte and reran the measured-response suite. [Drive probes](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/collab/clones.md#L1584-L1607), [seven-stage capture](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/collab/clones.md#L1173-L1175), [landed four-instance optimization](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/collab/clones.md#L892-L896).
- **Crush.** The build used steps at 27 static bit heights and signed markers over rates of 44.1, 48 and 96 kHz to isolate quantization and sample holding. The original moving musical setting had a **0.09781** peak error. The resolution-crossing repair now in main brought nine transition fixtures down from **0.032832** to **0.00000166** worst RMS. On the registered moving-bit kick-and-bass fixture, left and right RMS fell to **0.000231** and **0.000499**. Independently, calibrated capture timing brought the worst of twelve moving hold fixtures to **0.00000173 RMS** from as much as **0.01293**, and the block-grid repair made isolated Crush sample-identical across the tested block partitions. The fixed eight-point curve still leaves documented edge residuals on some music fixtures. [Static methods](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/collab/clones.md#L1609-L1621), [early music comparison](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/collab/clones.md#L1443-L1447), [moving bits](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/collab/clones.md#L509-L515), [hold clock and edge residual](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/collab/clones.md#L415-L427), [block-grid regression](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/collab/clones.md#L820-L824).

## SC-482 — OneShot and Live 12 Simpler

**19 `has` · 28 `part` · 42 `missing` · 0 `out`**

Sheet 7 puts the waveform and its flags, playback mode, Loop and the large Volume control on the face. Its opened Sound section shows Retrig and Gate. The current window uses a generic parameter panel and sample tile. [Sheet 7, opened view and face](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/b65fb0bac200689a9464168932e6e8731517ef4c/adi_daw/collab/window/design/proposals/devices-7.html#L21-L55) · [Sheet 7, face and layout notes](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/b65fb0bac200689a9464168932e6e8731517ef4c/adi_daw/collab/window/design/proposals/devices-7.html#L76-L95) · [Current panel](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/juce/ui_devices.cpp#L1585-L1620).

Live 12 §31.11 links to Warp Modes for the algorithm details. In-tree tests cover the behavior cited below. Live 12.4’s default preset pins shared control metadata, with matched audio renders still owed for the laws identified in the table. DEV-21 cites ADR-0169 d4 for the specifically named OneShot features. [Simpler](https://www.ableton.com/en/live-manual/12/live-instrument-reference/#simpler) · [Warp modes](https://www.ableton.com/en/live-manual/12/audio-clips-tempo-and-warping/#warp-modes) · [ADR-0169 d4](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/docs/DECISIONS.md#L11677-L11690).

| What the model does | What ADI does today | Where it would sit on the card | State | A test | Existing plan row |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **[Sample display and loading](https://www.ableton.com/en/live-manual/12/live-instrument-reference/#simpler)** | | | | | |
| Drop a sample from the browser or desktop onto the waveform to load it. | The tile assigns the file to slot 1, and the native instrument reads the prepared sample. [canvas 12](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/pd/devices/OneShot.pd#L12), [Device View 471–480](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/juce/ui_devices.cpp#L471-L480), [native adapter 361–368](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/pd_builtins/native_devices.cpp#L361-L368), [window test 5878–5902](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/tests/test_ui_shell.cpp#L5878-L5902), [instrument test 873–922](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/tests/test_builtin_devices.cpp#L873-L922). | Face · waveform drop target. | `has` | Drop a known WAV onto the empty tile and play its first cycle at the root note. | — |
| Replace the current sample by dropping another file or using Hot-Swap. | File drop replaces the selected sample, and the sample tile launches a slot-specific Hot-Swap. The Pd runtime checks replacement. The shell exercises the sample browser. [Device View 471–480](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/juce/ui_devices.cpp#L471-L480), [Device View 1585–1601](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/juce/ui_devices.cpp#L1585-L1601), [Pd test 142–152](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/tests/test_oneshot_pd.cpp#L142-L152), [window test 5871–5902](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/tests/test_ui_shell.cpp#L5871-L5902). | Face · Hot-Swap beside the waveform. | `has` | Replace a sounding sine with a square through Hot-Swap and verify that the next note reads the square. | — |
| Load an audio clip into the instrument with its clip or loop bounds and Warp settings intact. | A sample file can fill OneShot on a MIDI track. The drop command accepts a file path and assigns that source. [project document 2461–2478](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/ui/project_document.cpp#L2461-L2478), [document test 1377–1405](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/tests/test_ui_document.cpp#L1377-L1405). | Face · waveform drop target. | `part` | Drop a trimmed, warped clip into OneShot and verify the imported region, loop bounds, mode and marker timing. | [DEV-34 978](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/docs/PLAN.md#L978) covers the clip's range and names instrument Warp as a dependency. Loop and marker transfer have no specific row. |
| Expand the waveform into a larger view while keeping sound controls accessible. | The current panel creates a sample tile and generic parameter entries. [Device View 1585–1619](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/juce/ui_devices.cpp#L1585-L1619). | Face · arrow to opened view. | `missing` | Open the waveform, confirm its area grows and change Transpose while it stays open. | no row. |
| Zoom and pan the waveform around a small part of the sample in each mode. | Nothing. Waveform pixels span the full sample, and its mouse handler focuses the tile. [Device View 414–419](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/juce/ui_devices.cpp#L414-L419), [Device View 453–468](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/juce/ui_devices.cpp#L453-L468). | Opened · waveform. | `missing` | Zoom to a single transient and drag to a later point in each playback mode. | no row. |

| Switch between Classic, One-Shot and Slicing playback. | All three paths are declared and exercised by audio checks. [canvas 18–19](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/pd/devices/OneShot.pd#L18-L19), [DSP 379–400](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/dsp/oneshot.cpp#L379-L400), [DSP test 522–578](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/tests/test_oneshot.cpp#L522-L578). | Face · joined playback mode buttons. | `has` | Switch through all three modes and verify a held loop, a one-pass hit and a keyed slice. | — |
| Set an absolute start and end and then choose a start offset and playable length within those flags. | Start and End are absolute percentages, and Length scales their interval. A separate relative Start inside the flagged region is absent. The current tile draws samples without draggable flags. [canvas 20–25](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/pd/devices/OneShot.pd#L20-L25), [DSP 382–389](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/dsp/oneshot.cpp#L382-L389), [Device View 414–419](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/juce/ui_devices.cpp#L414-L419). | Face · waveform flags. Opened · Start and Length. | `part` | Mark frames 100–900, set a 25% relative Start and a 50% Length, then compare playback against the corresponding source indexes. | [DEV-21 970](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/docs/PLAN.md#L970) via ADR-0169 d4 covers the named feature. |
| Sustain a held note with the loop switch and choose how much of the playable region repeats. | Loop On wraps from end to the selected loop start and continues through release. [canvas 26–29](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/pd/devices/OneShot.pd#L26-L29), [DSP 429–445](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/dsp/oneshot.cpp#L429-L445), [DSP 611–625](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/dsp/oneshot.cpp#L611-L625), [DSP test 576–578](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/tests/test_oneshot.cpp#L576-L578), [DSP test 673–695](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/tests/test_oneshot.cpp#L673-L695). | Face · Loop and waveform loop bar. Opened · Loop Length. | `has` | Hold a note past the end of a short sample, then switch the loop off and verify the sample plays once. | — |
| Snap playback and loop markers to zero crossings on a stereo sample's left channel. | Preparation finds first-channel sign crossings and the voice snaps bounds to the next indexed crossing. The current test checks audible start, with no loop and stereo assertion. [DSP 191–205](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/dsp/oneshot.cpp#L191-L205), [DSP 432–444](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/dsp/oneshot.cpp#L432-L444), [DSP test 663–670](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/tests/test_oneshot.cpp#L663-L670). | Opened · Snap by the waveform. | `part` | Give the two channels different zero crossings and verify the start, end and loop follow the left-channel positions. | [DEV-21 970](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/docs/PLAN.md#L970) via ADR-0169 d4 covers the named feature. |
| Crossfade the join between a loop's end and start. | A constant-power blend smooths the join, with the overlap skipped on wrap. [DSP 615–620](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/dsp/oneshot.cpp#L615-L620), [DSP 681–698](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/dsp/oneshot.cpp#L681-L698), [DSP test 663–668](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/tests/test_oneshot.cpp#L663-L668). | Opened · Loop Fade by the waveform. | `has` | Loop a discontinuity with and without Fade and compare the largest step across the join. | — |
| Set the voice limit and let new notes replace the oldest voices after that limit. | The core is polyphonic and picks the oldest voice when no voice is free. The tests verify polyphony and a one-voice cap, while the Live chooser's exact list is a pending reading. [canvas 34–35](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/pd/devices/OneShot.pd#L34-L35), [DSP 399–412](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/dsp/oneshot.cpp#L399-L412), [DSP test 640–660](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/tests/test_oneshot.cpp#L640-L660), [device notes](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/pd/devices/OneShot.md#L14-L17). | Opened · Sound, Voices. | `part` | At each of Live's voice choices, exceed the limit with distinct sustained pitches and check which notes survive. | [DEV-21 970](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/docs/PLAN.md#L970) via ADR-0169 d4 covers the named feature. |
| Retrig can restart an already playing pitch or let repeated notes of that pitch overlap. | The native allocator ends same-pitch voices with Retrigger on. The Pd fallback releases an older same-channel/key identity even with it off. No native on/off comparison test was found. [DSP 399–412](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/dsp/oneshot.cpp#L399-L412), [Pd MIDI 35–43](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/pd_builtins/oneshot_midi.hpp#L35-L43), [DSP test 339–352](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/tests/test_oneshot.cpp#L339-L352). | Opened · Sound, Retrig. | `part` | Repeat the same key with two voices and compare its overlapping amplitudes with Retrig off and on. | [DEV-21 970](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/docs/PLAN.md#L970) via ADR-0169 d4 covers the named feature. |
| Adjust source Gain separately from the instrument's output Volume in every mode. | The canvas declares both controls and the core multiplies their gains in the voice. The existing modulation check isolates Volume but does not isolate both stages together. [canvas 50–53](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/pd/devices/OneShot.pd#L50-L53), [DSP 569–573](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/dsp/oneshot.cpp#L569-L573), [DSP 676–707](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/dsp/oneshot.cpp#L676-L707), [DSP test 142–195](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/tests/test_oneshot.cpp#L142-L195). | Face · large Volume. Opened · Sound, Sample Gain. | `part` | In all three modes, change each gain independently and check the expected output ratio and final Volume response. | no row. |

| Play a fixed region once with one voice and without Classic's Loop, Length or Voices settings. | Mode 1 uses Start and End, a one-voice allocator and the non-looping path. The test verifies mono. The panel builds common controls from its declaration list. | Keep playing the selected region after releasing a note in Trigger mode. | Gate off ignores note-off outside Classic. The regression renders through a released key and pedal. [canvas 38–39](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/pd/devices/OneShot.pd#L38-L39), [DSP 482–490](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/dsp/oneshot.cpp#L482-L490), [DSP test 244–249](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/tests/test_oneshot.cpp#L244-L249). | Opened · Sound, Gate/Trigger. | `has` | Release a triggered note early and verify it plays to the selected end. | — |

| **[Playback modes · Slicing](https://www.ableton.com/en/live-manual/12/live-instrument-reference/#slicing-playback-mode)** | | | | | |
| Use start and end flags to limit which slices can play. | Start and End constrain slice playback, and tests prove no playback for out-of-range starts and truncation at End. The tile has no movable markers. [DSP 348–390](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/dsp/oneshot.cpp#L348-L390), [DSP test 459–475](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/tests/test_oneshot.cpp#L459-L475), [Device View 414–419](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/juce/ui_devices.cpp#L414-L419). | Face · waveform flags. | `part` | Drag the flags across three slices and verify which keys remain playable and where the last one stops. | no row. |
| Automatically slice transients and vary the detection sensitivity up to 64 slices. | Preparation finds at most 64 onsets with fixed level and time thresholds. The audio test plays a detected onset. [DSP 191–205](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/dsp/oneshot.cpp#L191-L205), [DSP 364–372](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/dsp/oneshot.cpp#L364-L372), [DSP test 564–569](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/tests/test_oneshot.cpp#L564-L569). | Opened · Slice By and Sensitivity by the waveform. | `part` | Increase Sensitivity on an alternating soft/loud break and check that weaker onsets appear and the count stops at 64. | [DEV-21 970](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/docs/PLAN.md#L970) via ADR-0169 d4 covers the named feature. Sensitivity has no specific row. |
| Split the playable region at a selected beat division. | Beat uses the sample BPM and selected division. A rendered slice checks the expected source segment. [canvas 44–49](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/pd/devices/OneShot.pd#L44-L49), [DSP 355–362](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/dsp/oneshot.cpp#L355-L362), [DSP test 558–562](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/tests/test_oneshot.cpp#L558-L562). | Opened · Slice By, Division and sample Tempo. | `has` | Switch the division from a quarter to an eighth and verify the second slice starts at the new beat interval. | — |
| Split the playback region into a chosen number of equal slices. | Region divides the selected span by the requested count, and a test checks the second region's start and end. [canvas 40–43](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/pd/devices/OneShot.pd#L40-L43), [DSP 355–362](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/dsp/oneshot.cpp#L355-L362), [DSP test 547–555](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/tests/test_oneshot.cpp#L547-L555). | Opened · Slice By and Regions. | `has` | Split a ramp into four regions and verify each of the four keys starts one quarter further into it. | — |
| Add hand-placed slices by double-clicking the waveform in Manual mode. | The DSP plays a supplied marker list, while production sample assignment calls preparation with the default empty list and its tile lacks marker editing. [DSP 167–189](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/dsp/oneshot.cpp#L167-L189), [DSP 364–372](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/dsp/oneshot.cpp#L364-L372), [sample loader 204–215](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/juce/builtin_samples.cpp#L204-L215), [DSP test 570–574](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/tests/test_oneshot.cpp#L570-L574). | Opened · slice markers on the waveform. | `part` | Double-click a non-grid crossing, reopen the project and verify the assigned key starts exactly at the stored marker. | no row. |

## SC-483 — Sampler and Live 12 Sampler

**7 `has` · 55 `part` · 54 `missing` · 0 `out`**

The reference is Ableton’s Live 12 Instrument Reference §31.10. The placement proposals use Sheet 7’s keyboard map and selected sample waveform, with Volume prominent and detailed editing behind the arrow. The built-in instantiates the 32-slot adapter. `publishSlotZones` has DSP consumers and test callers, while the adapter only supplies prepared audio and parameters. This leaves the public editor and project persistence of nondefault zones unfinished. [Live 12 Sampler](https://www.ableton.com/en/live-manual/12/live-instrument-reference/#sampler) · [Sheet 7](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/b65fb0bac200689a9464168932e6e8731517ef4c/adi_daw/collab/window/design/proposals/devices-7.html#L56-L95) · [Built-in adapter](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/pd_builtins/native_devices.cpp#L379-L430) · [Zone publication](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/dsp/sampler.cpp#L122-L135) · [Slot publication test](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/tests/test_sampler.cpp#L214-L236)

Opened view · rate mode and beat division | part | Change tempo during playback and verify the LFO follows it only in sync mode. | [DEV-21](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/docs/PLAN.md#L3664) · LFOs in [ADR-0169 d4](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/docs/DECISIONS.md#L11683) |
| Fade modulation in with an adjustable LFO attack. | Nothing scales modulation with a dedicated attack. The LFO output enters each destination directly. [LFO and gain paths](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/dsp/oneshot.cpp#L649-L677) | Opened view · LFO Attack | missing | Measure the depth on successive cycles from note-on until the selected attack time elapses. | [DEV-21](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/docs/PLAN.md#L3664) · LFO family in [ADR-0169 d4](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/docs/DECISIONS.md#L11683). No row for attack. |
| Choose whether each note restarts an LFO or leaves it free-running. | Voice initialization zeroes the phase at every onset and no retrigger switch exists for the LFO. [Voice initialization](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/dsp/oneshot.cpp#L399-L428) · [Phase advancement](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/dsp/oneshot.cpp#L649-L664) | Opened view · LFO Retrig | part | Play two notes a quarter cycle apart and verify they start at equal or different phases according to Retrig. | [DEV-21](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/docs/PLAN.md#L3664) · LFO family in [ADR-0169 d4](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/docs/DECISIONS.md#L11683). No row for retrigger. |
| Set the starting phase of a retriggered LFO. | Nothing changes the phase established when the voice is reset. [Voice initialization](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/dsp/oneshot.cpp#L413-L428) · [LFO phase](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/dsp/oneshot.cpp#L649-L664) | Opened view · LFO Offset | missing | Compare the first quarter-cycle at phase zero and at a quarter-cycle offset. | [DEV-21](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/docs/PLAN.md#L3664) · LFO family in [ADR-0169 d4](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/docs/DECISIONS.md#L11683). No row for phase offset. |
| Increase each LFO’s rate with keyboard pitch. | Nothing applies keyboard pitch to the LFO rate, which is computed once from the global rate or period. [LFO rate](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/dsp/oneshot.cpp#L574-L579) | Opened view · LFO Rate from Key | missing | Play two distant notes and measure their modulation periods with key tracking on and off. | [DEV-21](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/docs/PLAN.md#L3664) · LFO family in [ADR-0169 d4](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/docs/DECISIONS.md#L11683). No row for key tracking. |
| Set LFO 1 depth separately for volume, pan, cutoff and pitch. | The four depths are implemented, with per-voice gain and channel balance, but the tests cover audible volume and pitch rather than every target or its Live range. [Declarations](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/sampler_declarations.hpp#L31-L34) · [Cutoff](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/dsp/oneshot.cpp#L561-L567) · [Pitch, gain and pan](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/dsp/oneshot.cpp#L666-L709) · [Pitch test](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/tests/test_sampler.cpp#L128-L160) · [Volume test](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/tests/test_oneshot.cpp#L588-L591) | Opened view · four LFO 1 depth knobs | part | Turn up each depth alone and verify only its output target varies at the chosen rate. | [DEV-21](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/docs/PLAN.md#L3664) · LFOs in [ADR-0169 d4](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/docs/DECISIONS.md#L11683) |

No row for stereo modes. |
| Route LFOs 2 and 3 to two selectable targets each, including modulation controls. | Nothing exposes a per-voice destination matrix or self-modulation in Sampler. The single LFO writes four fixed targets. | Route the played key to two selectable targets at independent amounts. | Key controls the fixed playback pitch and layer admission, with no selectable key-to-parameter routing. | **Importing Third-Party Multisamples** | | | | | |
| Import the documented REX, ACID or Soundtrack Loops sources as playable material. | The sample tile takes ordinary audio through the assignment path. Nothing in the adapter imports these formats as mapped Sampler layers. [Sample tile actions](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/juce/ui_devices.cpp#L308-L345) · [Adapter](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/pd_builtins/native_devices.cpp#L416-L430) | Menu · Browser import to Sampler | missing | Import one supported example of each type and verify the expected layer material can be triggered. | no row for Sampler import. [TM-MD-05](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/docs/PLAN.md#L1087) covers general decoding and records no usable REX path |
| Save imported mappings as reusable presets in an Imports category. | ADI can list and reload its native device presets, but no importer builds mapped presets or an Imports category for Sampler. [Preset capture](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/ui/project_document.cpp#L1974-L2004) · [Browser preset test](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/tests/test_ui_shell.cpp#L4705-L4743) | Menu · Browser Imports | part | Import one mapping, reopen the application and launch the imported preset from its Browser category. | no row |
| Convert imports that need several Samplers into one playable instrument rack. | Nothing converts a third-party multisample to a mapped rack. [Native preset load](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/ui/project_document.cpp#L2040-L2058) · [Built-in adapter](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/src/adi/pd_builtins/native_devices.cpp#L416-L430) | Menu · Browser import | missing | Import a source requiring multiple layers of processing and verify its reconstructed rack reproduces the fixture’s routing. | no row for multisample conversion. [DEV-32](https://github.com/arieladi/Adi/blob/a916c47c1d1e01aee9c70824e95c3e592a35c3c0/adi_daw/docs/PLAN.md#L976) covers reading the director’s existing `.adg` racks |

The Live 12 MIDI-tab figure shows Note PB, Slide and Pressure controls. Ableton’s Sampler release notes explain their behavior, and its MPE FAQ confirms current Live 12 support. [Live 12 MIDI tab](https://www.ableton.com/en/live-manual/12/live-instrument-reference/#the-midi-tab) · [Live MIDI tab figure](https://ableton-production.imgix.net/live-manual/12/SamplerMIDITabL12.png?auto=&w=1410) · [Sampler MPE release notes](https://www.ableton.com/en/release-notes/live-11/) · [MPE in Live FAQ](https://help.ableton.com/hc/en-us/articles/360019144999-MPE-in-Live-FAQ)
